STRATEGY (15-minute, 3-minute and 1-minute candles)
Read on the previous day, the latest completed session. Candles are labelled by start time: the last candles are 15m 15:15 (second last 15:00), 3m 15:27 (second last 15:24) and 1m 15:29 (second last 15:28). A stock matches only when ALL of these hold:
1) 15m trend: the 15:00 and 15:15 candles are in opposite trends (one Up, one Down).
2) 15m volume: the 15:00 candle's volume is higher than the 15:15 candle's.
3) 3m volume: the 15:24 candle's volume is higher than the 15:27 candle's.
4) 1m volume: the 15:28 candle's volume is higher than the 15:29 candle's.
5) The 15m 15:00, 3m 15:24 and 1m 15:28 candles all have the same trend.
The 3m and 1m timeframes have no opposite-trend rule: their last two candles may have the same or opposite trends.
Trend = candle colour (close vs open); a flat candle has no trend, so it is never opposite or the same. Direction follows the alignment trend (the second-last candles: 15m 15:00 / 3m 15:24 / 1m 15:28): Up is LONG, Down is SHORT.
Entry: the 09:15 open on entry day. Exit: 15:27.

Data notes: 15-minute and 1-minute candles come straight from the data source's own feeds (NSE's charting feed, or Yahoo as a fallback - shown per stock in the Data column); there is no 3-minute feed, so 3-minute candles are built from the 1-minute candles on a 09:15 grid (open = first, close = last, volume = sum). Prices are rounded to 2 decimals; zero-volume and corrupt candles are dropped and count as missing, which can only block a match. DATA CONFLICT means a native 15m candle's trend, or which 15m candle has more volume, disagrees with the same candles rebuilt from the 1m candles inside them, so the symbol is blocked rather than trusted. INCOMPLETE means no completed session (or none of the six candles) came back. STALE means the previous day differs from most other symbols. Each candle is shown as open→close with its volume so a signal can be checked against a chart.
